As I placed both hands on the case of the apparently frail little instrument, he added, “You remember how easily you moved it just a moment ago.”

===Chinese===

我把双手按在这个看似单薄的小仪器的外壳上，他又补了一句：「还记得你刚才把它转得多么轻巧吧。」

===Original===

I pressed down with all my might. Then I literally raised myself off my feet, and my whole weight was on the gyroscope. That uncanny little instrument seemed to resent﻿—yes, that’s the word, resent﻿—my touch. It was almost human in the resentment, too. Far from yielding to me, it actually rose on the side I was pressing down!

===Chinese===

我用尽全身力气往下压，接着干脆把双脚离了地，把整个身子的重量都吊在陀螺仪上。那小仪器透着一股邪性，仿佛在憎恶——对，就是憎恶这个词——我的触碰；那份憎恶几乎带着人味儿。它非但不肯向我屈服，反倒在我下压的那一侧抬了起来！

===Original===

The men who were watching me laughed at the puzzled look on my face.

===Chinese===

旁边看我表演的几个人瞧见我一脸茫然，都笑了。

===Original===

I took my hands off, and the gyroscope leisurely and nonchalantly went back to its original position.

===Chinese===

我把手撒开，陀螺仪不慌不忙、若无其事地回到了原先的位置。

===Original===

“That’s the property we use, applied to the rudder and the ailerons﻿—those flat planes between the large main planes. That gives automatic stability to the machine,” continued Norton. “I’m not going to explain how it is done﻿—it is in the combination of the various parts that I have discovered the basic principle, and I’m not going to talk about it till the thing is settled by the courts. But it is there, and the court will see it, and I’ll prove that Delanne is a fraud﻿—a fraud when he says that my combination isn’t patentable and isn’t practicable even at that. The truth is that his device as it stands isn’t practicable, and, besides, if he makes it so it infringes on mine. Would you like to take a flight with me?”

===Chinese===

「我们利用的正是这种特性，把它用在方向舵和副翼上——就是大主机翼之间的那些小平板。这样飞机就获得了自动稳定性，」诺顿接着说。「具体怎么实现，我不打算解释——我的基本原理就藏在各部件的组合之中，在法院了结此案之前，我一个字也不会多说。但它是实实在在的，法庭自会看到，而且我要证明德兰是个骗子——他说我的组合不能取得专利、即便取得也不切实用，这话就是骗人。实情是，他那套装置照现在的样子才是不切实用的；退一步说，他真要改得切实用了，那也侵犯了我的专利。愿意跟我飞一趟吗？」

===Original===

I looked at Kennedy, and a vision of the wreckage of the two previous accidents, as the *Star* photographer had snapped them, flashed across my mind. But Kennedy was too quick for me.

===Chinese===

我望了望肯尼迪，脑子里闪过《明星报》摄影记者拍下的前两次事故的残骸。可肯尼迪抢在了我前头。

===Original===

“Yes,” he answered. “A short flight. No stunts.”

===Chinese===

「愿意，」他答道。「飞一小段。不许玩花样。」

===Original===

We took our seats by Norton, I, at least, with some misgiving. Gently the machine rose into the air. The sensation was delightful. The fresh air of the morning came with a stinging rush to my face. Below I could see the earth sweeping past as if it were a moving-picture film. Above the continuous roar of the engine and propeller Norton indicated to Kennedy the automatic balancing of the gyroscope as it bent the ailerons.

===Chinese===

我们在诺顿身旁坐定——至少我心里是带着几分忐忑的。飞机轻盈地升入空中。那感觉妙极了。清晨的新鲜空气扑面而来，扎得人脸颊发麻。俯视下方，大地像一卷活动的电影胶片似的不住掠过。在引擎和螺旋桨持续不断的轰鸣之上，诺顿指给肯尼迪看陀螺仪如何操纵副翼、自动保持平衡。

===Original===

“Could you fly in this machine without the gyroscope at all?” yelled Kennedy. The noise was deafening, conversation almost impossible. Though sitting side by side he had to repeat his remark twice to Norton.

===Chinese===

「这架飞机要是根本不带陀螺仪，你还能飞吗？」肯尼迪大声喊道。噪声震耳欲聋，交谈几乎不可能。两人虽是并肩而坐，他这句话还是对诺顿重复了两遍。

===Original===

“Yes,” called back Norton. Reaching back of him, he pointed out the way to detach the gyroscope and put a sort of brake on it that stopped its revolutions almost instantly. “It’s a ticklish job to change in the air,” he shouted. “It can be done, but it’s safer to land and do it.”

===Chinese===

「能，」诺顿回头喊道。他把手伸到身后，比划着如何把陀螺仪卸下来，再给它加上一种闸，几乎能立刻刹住它的旋转。「在空中干这活儿可悬，」他喊道，「干是干得了，可还是降落下来再干稳妥。」

===Original===

The flight was soon over, and we stood admiring the machine while Norton expatiated on the compactness of his little dynamo.

===Chinese===

飞行很快结束了。我们站着欣赏这架飞机，诺顿则滔滔不绝地夸他那台小发电机的紧凑。

===Original===

“What have you done with the wrecks of the other machines?” inquired Kennedy at length.

===Chinese===

「另外两架飞机的残骸，你是怎么处置的？」肯尼迪终于问道。

===Original===

“They are stored in a shed down near the railroad station. They are just a mass of junk, though there are some parts that I can use, so I’ll ship them back to the factory.”

===Chinese===

「存放在火车站附近下坡处的一间棚子里。纯粹是一堆废铁了，不过有些零件还用得上，回头我把它们运回厂里去。」

===Original===

“Might I have a look at them?”

===Chinese===

「我能去看看吗？」

===Original===

“Surely. I’ll give you the key. Sorry I can’t go myself, but I want to be sure everything is all right for my flight this afternoon.”

===Chinese===

「当然可以。钥匙给你。抱歉我不能亲自陪你去，下午还要飞行，我得确保一切万无一失。」

===Original===

It was a long walk over to the shed near the station, and, together with our examination of the wrecked machines, it took us the rest of the morning. Craig carefully turned over the wreckage. It seemed a hopeless quest to me, but I fancied that to him it merely presented new problems for his deductive and scientific mind.

===Chinese===

到车站旁那间棚子去路不近，加上察看失事飞机的残骸，我们把上午剩下的时间全用光了。克雷格细细翻检着残骸。在我看来，这差事毫无指望；可我料想，在他眼里，这不过是给他的演绎头脑和科学头脑又出了几道新题。

===Original===

“These gyroscopes are out of business for good,” he remarked as he glanced at the dented and battered aluminum cases. “But there doesn’t seem to be anything wrong with them except what would naturally happen in such accidents.”

===Chinese===

「这些陀螺仪是彻底报废了，」他瞥着那些撞瘪碰伤的铝壳说。「不过除了这种事故中难免的损伤，它们本身看不出有什么毛病。」

===Original===

For my part I felt a sort of awe at the mass of wreckage in which Browne and Herrick had been killed. It was to me more than a tangled mass of wires and splinters. Two human lives had been snuffed out in it.

===Chinese===

至于我，面对这堆夺去布朗和赫里克性命的残骸，心里生出一种敬畏。在我眼里，它绝不只是一团乱糟糟的钢丝和破木片——两条人命就在这里面熄灭了。

===Original===

“The engines are a mass of scrap; see how the cylinders are bent and twisted,” remarked Kennedy with great interest. “The gasoline-tank is intact, but dented out of shape. No explosion there. And look at this dynamo. Why, the wires in it are actually fused together. The insulation has been completely burned off. I wonder what could have caused that?”

===Chinese===

「发动机成了一堆废料；瞧这些汽缸，都撞弯拧歪了，」肯尼迪饶有兴味地说。「油箱倒是完好，只是撞瘪变了形——没有发生过爆炸。再看这台发电机。咦，里头的电线竟然熔结在一起了。绝缘层烧得精光。是什么造成的呢，真叫人纳闷。」

===Original===

Kennedy continued to regard the tangled mass thoughtfully for some time, then locked the door, and we strolled back to the grand stand on our side of the field. Already the crowd had begun to collect. Across the field we could see the various machines in front of their hangars with the men working on them. The buzz of the engines was wafted across by the light summer breeze as if a thousand cicadas had broken loose to predict warm weather.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪对着那团乱麻沉思了好一会儿，然后锁上门，我们缓步走回场地这一侧的大看台。人群已经开始聚集。隔着飞行场，能望见一架架飞机停在各自的机库前，机械师们在机上忙活。引擎的嗡鸣乘着夏日轻轻的微风飘过来，仿佛上千只蝉挣脱了出来，预告着大热天。

===Original===

Two machines were already in flight, a little yellow Demoiselle, scurrying around close to the earth like a frightened hen, and a Bleriot, high overhead, making slow and graceful turns like a huge bird.

===Chinese===

已经有两架飞机在飞：一架小小的黄色德穆瓦泽尔（Demoiselle），贴着地面打转，活像一只受惊的母鸡；一架布莱里奥（Bleriot）高高在上，缓缓地、优雅地兜着圈子，像一只巨鸟。

===Original===

Kennedy and I stopped before the little wireless telegraph station of the signal corps in front of the grand stand and watched the operator working over his instruments.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪和我在大看台前通信兵的小无线电报站旁停下，看报务员摆弄他的仪器。

===Original===

“There it is again,” muttered the operator angrily.

===Chinese===

「又来了。」报务员气恼地咕哝。

===Original===

“What’s the matter?” asked Kennedy. “Amateurs interfering with you?”

===Chinese===

「怎么回事？」肯尼迪问。「业余台在干扰你？」

===Original===

The man nodded a reply, shaking his head with the telephone-like receiver, viciously. He continued to adjust his apparatus.

===Chinese===

那人点点头算是回答，脑袋上戴着那副电话似的收报机，狠狠地晃着。他继续调他的设备。

===Original===

“Confound it!” he exclaimed. “Yes, that fellow has been jamming me for the past two days off and on, every time I get ready to send or receive a message. Williams is going up with a Wright machine equipped with wireless apparatus in a minute, and this fellow won’t get out of the way. By Jove, though, those are powerful impulses of his. Hear that crackling? I’ve never been interfered with so in my experience. Touch that screen door with your knife.”

===Chinese===

「见鬼！」他嚷道。「没错，这家伙断断续续干扰我两天了，每回我刚要发电报或收电报，他就来。威廉斯（Williams）马上要驾一架装着无线电设备的莱特式飞机上天，这家伙偏偏不肯让开。乖乖，他那电波可真够强的。听见那噼啪声没有？我干这行还从没受过这么厉害的干扰。拿你的小刀碰碰那扇纱门。」

===Original===

Kennedy did so, and elicited large sparks with quite a tingle of a shock.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪依言照办，刀尖碰上门去，立刻爆出好大的火花，震得他一阵发麻。

===Original===

“Yesterday and the day before it was so bad we had to give up attempting to communicate with Williams,” continued the operator. “It was worse than trying to work in a thundershower. That’s the time we get our troubles, when the air is overcharged with electricity, as it is now.”

===Chinese===

「昨天和前天糟得我们只好放弃同威廉斯的联络，」报务员接着说。「比在雷雨天里干活还难受。空气里电荷过饱和的时候，正是我们吃苦头的时候——眼下就是这样。」

===Original===

“That’s interesting,” remarked Kennedy.

===Chinese===

「这很有意思。」肯尼迪说。

===Original===

“Interesting?” flashed back the operator, angrily noting the condition in his log book.

===Chinese===

「有意思？」报务员回了一句，一边气呼呼地把这情况记进值机日志。

===Original===

“Maybe it is, but I call it darned mean. It’s almost like trying to work in a power station.”

===Chinese===

「也许吧，反正我说这缺德透了。简直像在发电厂里拍电报。」

===Original===

“Indeed?” queried Kennedy. “I beg your pardon﻿—I was only looking at it from the purely scientific point of view. Who is it, do you suppose?”

===Chinese===

「是吗？」肯尼迪问。「恕我失言——我只是从纯科学的角度看这件事。你说，会是谁呢？」

===Original===

“How do I know? Some amateur, I guess. No professional would butt in this way.”

===Chinese===

「我哪知道？八成是哪个业余的。干这行的谁也不会这么蛮不讲理地硬插进来。」

===Original===

Kennedy took a leaf out of his notebook and wrote a short message which he gave to a boy to deliver to Norton.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪从笔记本上撕下一页，写了封短信，交给一个小家伙送去给诺顿。

===Original===

“Detach your gyroscope and dynamo,” it read. “Leave them in the hangar. Fly without them this afternoon, and see what happens. No use to try for the prize today. Kennedy.”

===Chinese===

短信上写着：「把陀螺仪和发电机卸下，留在机库里。今天下午不带它们飞，看看会怎样。今天不必去争那个奖了。肯尼迪。」

===Original===

We sauntered out on the open part of the field, back of the fence and to the side of the stands, and watched the fliers for a few moments. Three were in the air now, and I could see Norton and his men getting ready.

===Chinese===

我们溜达到场地开阔的那一片，在围栏后面、看台一侧，看了一会儿飞行。空中有三架飞机了，我还看得见诺顿和他手下的人在做准备。

===Original===

The boy with the message was going rapidly across the field. Kennedy was impatiently watching him. It was too far off to see just what they were doing, but as Norton seemed to get down out of his seat in the aeroplane when the boy arrived, and it was wheeled back into the shed, I gathered that he was detaching the gyroscope and was going to make the flight without it, as Kennedy had requested.

===Chinese===

送信的孩子正飞快地穿过场地。肯尼迪焦躁地望着他。隔得太远，看不清那边到底在干什么，但那孩子一到，诺顿似乎就从飞机座舱里下来了，飞机也被推回了棚屋，我估摸他是在卸陀螺仪，打算照肯尼迪的要求，不带它上天。

===Original===

In a few minutes it was again wheeled out.

===Chinese===

不几分钟，飞机又推了出来。

===Original===

The crowd, which had been waiting especially to see Norton, applauded.

===Chinese===

专程等着看诺顿飞行的人群鼓起掌来。

===Original===

“Come, Walter,” exclaimed Kennedy, “let’s go up there on the roof of the stand where we can see better. There’s a platform and railing, I see.”

===Chinese===

「来，沃尔特，」肯尼迪喊道，「咱们上看台顶上去，那边看得更清楚。我瞧见上头有个平台，还带栏杆。」

===Original===

His pass allowed him to go anywhere on the field, so in a few moments we were up on the roof.

===Chinese===

他的通行证在场地内畅行无阻，不多时我们就登上了看台顶。

===Original===

It was a fascinating vantage-point, and I was so deeply engrossed between watching the crowd below, the bird-men in the air, and the machines waiting across the field that I totally neglected to notice what Kennedy was doing. When I did, I saw that he had deliberately turned his back on the aviation field, and was anxiously, scanning the country back of us.

===Chinese===

这观测点真叫人流连。我一会儿看底下的人群，一会儿看空中的飞行家，一会儿又看场地对面整装待发的飞机，看得入了神，压根儿没留意肯尼迪在干什么。等我留意到时，才发现他竟有意背对着飞行场，正急切地扫视我们身后的乡野。

===Original===

“What are you looking for?” I asked. “Turn around. I think Norton is just about to fly.”

===Chinese===

「你在找什么？」我问。「转过来吧。我看诺顿就要起飞了。」

===Original===

“Watch him then,” answered Craig. “Tell me when he gets in the air.”

===Chinese===

「那你就盯着他，」克雷格答道。「他一上了天就告诉我。」

===Original===

Just then Norton’s aeroplane rose gently from the field. A wild shout of applause came from the people below us, at the heroism of the man who dared to fly this new and apparently fated machine. It was succeeded by a breathless, deathly calm, as if after the first burst of enthusiasm the crowd had suddenly realised the danger of the intrepid aviator. Would Norton add a third to the fatalities of the meet?

===Chinese===

正在这时，诺顿的飞机轻盈地离了地。我们脚下的人群爆发出一阵狂热的喝彩，赞的是这位敢驾着新机器赴死的英雄——这机器瞧着简直像受了诅咒。喝彩过后，接踵而来的是屏息的死寂，仿佛一阵热情过后，人们猛然意识到这位勇士性命悬于一线。这届运动会的死亡名单上，诺顿会成为第三个吗？

===Original===

Suddenly Kennedy jerked my arm. “Walter, look over there across the road back of us﻿—at the old weather-beaten barn. I mean the one next to that yellow house. What do you see?”

===Chinese===

忽然，肯尼迪猛扯我的胳膊。「沃尔特，看我们身后马路对面——那座饱经风雨的旧谷仓。我是说黄房子旁边的那座。你看见什么了？」

===Original===

“Nothing, except that on the peak of the roof there is a pole that looks like the short stub of a small wireless mast. I should say there was a boy connected with that barn, a boy who has read a book on wireless for beginners.”

===Chinese===

「没什么，只除了屋脊上竖着一根竿子，像是小型无线电天线的短截。依我看，这谷仓八成住着个孩子——一个读过《无线电入门》一类的书的孩子。」

===Original===

“Maybe,” said Kennedy. “But is that all you see? Look up in the little window of the gable, the one with the closed shutter.”

===Chinese===

「也许吧，」肯尼迪说，「可你看见的就这些？再看看山墙上的那扇小窗——关着百叶的那扇。」

===Original===

I looked carefully. “It seems to me that I saw a gleam of something bright at the top of the shutter, Craig,” I ventured. “A spark or a flash.”

===Chinese===

我仔细瞧了瞧。「克雷格，我好像看见百叶窗上头闪过一点亮光，」我试探着说，「一个火花，或者一道闪光。」

===Original===

“It must be a bright spark, for the sun is shining brightly,” mused Craig.

===Chinese===

「那火花必定很亮，要知道太阳正毒着呢。」克雷格沉吟道。

===Original===

“Oh, maybe it’s the small boy with a looking-glass. I can remember when I used to get behind such a window and shine a glass into the darkened room of my neighbours across the street.”

===Chinese===

「哦，没准儿是那小孩子拿面小镜子晃的。我还记得自己小时候，就常躲在这样一扇窗户后面，拿镜子把光反射到街对面邻居拉暗了的房间里去。」

===Original===

I had really said that half in raillery, for I was at a loss to account in any other way for the light, but I was surprised to see how eagerly Craig accepted it.

===Chinese===

这话我半是打趣说的，因为那光亮实在没法作别的解释；不料克雷格竟那么急切地接受了这个说法，倒叫我吃了一惊。

===Original===

“Perhaps you are right, in a way,” he assented. “I guess it isn’t a spark, after all. Yes, it must be the reflection of the sun on a piece of glass﻿—the angles are just about right for it. Anyhow it caught my eye. Still, I believe that barn will bear watching.”

===Chinese===

「从某种意义上说，你也许是对的，」他表示同意。「我看那到底不是火花。是啊，准是太阳照在一块玻璃上的反光——角度恰好合适。反正它勾住了我的眼睛。不过，我认定那座谷仓值得盯着。」

===Original===

Whatever his suspicions, Craig kept them to himself, and descended. At the same time Norton gently dropped back to earth in front of his hangar, not ten feet from the spot where he started. The applause was deafening, as the machine was again wheeled into the shed safely.

===Chinese===

不管他起了什么疑心，克雷格都闷在心里，走下了看台。与此同时，诺顿轻轻地降回地面，落在他机库前面，离他起飞的地方不出十英尺。飞机又被平安推进棚屋，掌声震耳欲聋。

===Original===

Kennedy and I pushed through the crowd to the wireless operator.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪和我挤过人群，来到无线电报务员跟前。

===Original===

“How’s she working?” inquired Craig.

===Chinese===

「它现在好使了吗？」克雷格问。

===Original===

“Rotten,” replied the operator sullenly. “It was worse than ever about five minutes ago. It’s much better now, almost normal again.”

===Chinese===

「糟透了，」报务员没好气地答道。「五分钟前比什么时候都糟。现在好多了，差不多恢复正常了。」

===Original===

Just then the messenger-boy, who had been hunting through the crowd for us, handed Kennedy a note. It was merely a scrawl from Norton:

===Chinese===

正巧，那个一直在人群里找我们的信童递给肯尼迪一张字条，不过是诺顿潦草的几笔：

===Original===

“Everything seems fine. Am going to try her next with the gyroscope. **Norton**.”

===Chinese===

「一切正常。接下来准备带上陀螺仪再飞一趟。**诺顿**。」

===Original===

“Boy,” exclaimed Craig, “has Mr. Norton a telephone?”

===Chinese===

「孩子，」克雷格喊道，「诺顿先生有电话吗？」

===Original===

“No, sir, only that hangar at the end has a telephone.”

===Chinese===

「没有，先生。只有头上那座机库装了电话。」

===Original===

“Well, you run across that field as fast as your legs can carry you and tell him if he values his life not to do it.”

===Chinese===

「那好，你撒开腿用最快的速度穿过场地，告诉他，要是还惜命，就别那么干。」

===Original===

“Not to do what, sir?”

===Chinese===

「别干什么，先生？」

===Original===

“Don’t stand there, youngster. Run! Tell him not to fly with that gyroscope. There’s a five-spot in it if you get over there before he starts.”

===Chinese===

「别杵在那儿了，小家伙。跑！告诉他别带那陀螺仪飞。你要能在他起飞前赶到，有五美元赏你。」

===Original===

Even as he spoke the Norton aeroplane was wheeled out again. In a minute Norton had climbed up into his seat and was testing the levers.

===Chinese===

就在他说话的当口，诺顿的飞机又被推了出来。转眼间诺顿已经爬进座舱，试起操纵杆来。

===Original===

Would the boy reach him in time? He was half across the field, waving his arms like mad. But apparently Norton and his men were too engrossed in their machine to pay attention.

===Chinese===

孩子来得及赶到吗？他已经跑过半个场地，疯了似的挥舞胳膊。可诺顿和他手下的人显然全副心思都在机器上，根本无暇理会。

===Original===

“Good heavens!” exclaimed Craig. “He’s going to try it. Run, boy, run!” he cried, although the boy was now far out of hearing.

===Chinese===

「天哪！」克雷格喊了起来。「他真要飞了。跑啊，孩子，跑啊！」他大声嚷着，虽然那孩子早已跑得听不见了。

===Original===

Across the field we could hear now the quick staccato chug-chug of the engine. Slowly Norton’s aeroplane, this time really equipped with the gyroscope, rose from the field and circled over toward us. Craig frantically signalled to him to come down, but of course Norton could not have seen him in the crowd. As for the crowd, they looked askance at Kennedy, as if he had taken leave of his senses.

===Chinese===

隔着场地，我们此刻听得见引擎短促突突的爆发声。诺顿的飞机——这一回是真装上了陀螺仪——缓缓离地，朝我们这边盘旋过来。克雷格发疯似的打手势叫他下来，可处在人堆里，诺顿当然看不见他。至于观众们，都斜着眼瞧肯尼迪，只当他失了常。

===Original===

I heard the wireless operator cursing the way his receiver was acting.

===Chinese===

我听见无线电报务员在骂他的收报机抽的什么风。

===Original===

Higher and higher Norton went in one spiral after another, those spirals which his gyroscope had already made famous.

===Chinese===

诺顿盘旋而上，一圈高过一圈——正是靠陀螺仪而名闻遐迩的那手盘旋。

===Original===

The man with the megaphone in front of the judge’s stand announced in hollow tones that Mr. Norton had given notice that he would try for the Brooks Prize for stationary equilibrium.

===Chinese===

裁判台前拿着扩音筒的人用空洞的嗓音宣布：诺顿先生已照会大会，将冲击静止悬停项目的布鲁克斯奖。

===Original===

Kennedy and I stood speechless, helpless, appalled.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪和我站在那里，说不出话，帮不上忙，胆战心惊。

===Original===

Slower and slower went the aeroplane. It seemed to hover just like the big mechanical bird that it was.

===Chinese===

飞机越飞越慢，仿佛悬停在了半空——它本来就是一只硕大的机械鸟。

===Original===

Kennedy was anxiously watching the judges with one eye and Norton with the other. A few in the crowd could no longer restrain their applause. I remember that the wireless back of us was spluttering and crackling like mad.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪一只眼紧张地盯着裁判，另一只眼盯着诺顿。人群中已有几位忍不住喝起彩来。我还记得，我们身后的无线电正发疯似的噼啪乱响。

===Original===

All of a sudden a groan swept over the crowd. Something was wrong with Norton. His aeroplane was swooping downward at a terrific rate. Would he be able to control it? I held my breath and gripped Kennedy by the arm. Down, down came Norton, frantically fighting by main strength, it seemed to me, to warp the planes so that their surface might catch the air and check his descent.

===Chinese===

突然，人群里掠过一声惊呼。诺顿出事了。他的飞机正以骇人的速度向下俯冲。他还控制得住吗？我屏住呼吸，一把攥住肯尼迪的胳膊。诺顿越降越低、越降越低，在我看来，他正凭着一身蛮力拼命扭动机翼，好让翼面吃住气流，遏止下坠。

===Original===

“He’s trying to detach the gyroscope,” whispered Craig hoarsely.

===Chinese===

「他在试着卸陀螺仪。」克雷格嘶哑地低声说。

===Original===

The football helmet which Norton wore blew off and fell more rapidly than the plane. I shut my eyes. But Kennedy’s next exclamation caused me quickly to open them again.

===Chinese===

诺顿戴的橄榄球头盔被风吹脱，比飞机坠得更快。我闭上了眼睛。可肯尼迪随后一声惊呼，又叫我立刻睁开了眼。

===Original===

“He’ll make it, after all!”

===Chinese===

「他到底还是稳住了！」

===Original===

Somehow Norton had regained partial control of his machine, but it was still swooping down at a tremendous pace toward the level centre of the field.

===Chinese===

诺顿总算夺回了机器的一部分控制权，但它仍以惊人的速度朝场地中央的平地俯冲而下。

===Original===

There was a crash as it struck the ground in a cloud of dust.

===Chinese===

飞机撞上地面，在一团尘土中轰然巨响。

===Original===

With a leap Kennedy had cleared the fence and was running toward Norton. Two men from the judge’s stand were ahead of us, but except for them we were the first to reach him. The men were tearing frantically at the tangled framework, trying to lift it off Norton, who lay pale and motionless, pinned under it. The machine was not so badly damaged, after all, but that together we could lift it bodily off him.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪一跃翻过围栏，朝诺顿奔去。裁判台那边两个人跑在我们前头，但除了他们，就数我们最先赶到。人们发疯似的撕扯着扭成一团的骨架，想把它从诺顿身上抬起来——他脸色惨白，一动不动，被压在底下。机器总算坏得不算太重，我们合力把它整个从他身上抬了起来。

===Original===

A doctor ran out from the crowd and hastily put his ear to Norton’s chest. No one spoke, but we all scanned the doctor’s face anxiously.

===Chinese===

一位医生从人群中跑出来，急忙把耳朵贴在诺顿胸口。谁也不说话，大家都焦急地端详医生的脸色。

===Original===

“Just stunned﻿—he’ll be all right in a moment. Get some water,” he said.

===Chinese===

「只是震晕了——他马上就没事。拿点水来。」他说。

===Original===

Kennedy pulled my arm. “Look at the gyroscope dynamo,” he whispered.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪扯扯我的胳膊。「瞧那台陀螺仪发电机。」他悄声说。

===Original===

I looked. Like the other two which we had seen, it also was a wreck. The insulation was burned off the wires, the wires were fused together, and the storage-battery looked as if it had been burned out.

===Chinese===

我看了看。同我们见过的那两台一样，它也报废了。电线上的绝缘层烧光了，电线熔结在一起，蓄电池瞧着像是烧穿了。

===Original===

A flicker of the eyelid and Norton seemed to regain some degree of consciousness. He was living over again the ages that had passed during the seconds of his terrible fall.

===Chinese===

诺顿眼皮一颤，似乎恢复了几分知觉。那可怕坠落中的几秒钟，在他心里漫过了几个世纪，他正重新活一遍。

===Original===

“Will they never stop? Oh, those sparks, those sparks! I can’t disconnect it. Sparks, more sparks﻿—will they never﻿—” So he rambled on. It was fearsome to hear him.

===Chinese===

「它们就停不下来吗？哦，那些火花，那些火花！我拆不开它。火花，又是火花——它们就永远不——」他就这么语无伦次地说着。听他这样说，叫人毛骨悚然。

===Original===

But Kennedy was now sure that Norton was safe and in good hands, and he hurried back in the direction of the grand stand. I followed. Flying was over for that day, and the people were filing slowly out toward the railroad station where the special trains were waiting. We stopped at the wireless station for a moment.

===Chinese===

不过肯尼迪此刻已确信诺顿没有性命之虞，自有好人照看，便匆匆朝大看台方向赶回去。我跟在后面。当天的飞行就此结束，人们正鱼贯缓缓走向火车站，专列还在那里候着。我们在无线电台前停了停。

===Original===

“Is it true that Norton will recover?” inquired the operator.

===Chinese===

「诺顿真能脱险吗？」报务员问。

===Original===

“Yes. He was only stunned, thank Heaven! Did you keep a record of the antics of your receiver since I saw you last?”

===Chinese===

「是的。他只是震晕了，谢天谢地！从我上次见你到刚才，你的收报机那些反常举动，你都记录下来了？」

===Original===

“Yes, sir. And I made a copy for you. By the way, it’s working all right now when I don’t want it. If Williams was only in the air now I’d give you a good demonstration of communicating with an aeroplane,” continued the operator as he prepared to leave.

===Chinese===

「记了，先生。我还给您誊了一份。对了，如今我不需要它好使了，它倒好使起来了。要是威廉斯这会儿在天上，我就能好好给您演示一番怎么跟飞机联络。」报务员一面收拾要走，一面接着说。

===Original===

Kennedy thanked him for the record and carefully folded it. Joining the crowd, we pushed our way out, but instead of going down to the station with them, Kennedy turned toward the barn and the yellow house.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪谢过他送的记录，仔细折好收起。我们汇入人流，挤出场地，却没随大家下火车站，而是转身朝谷仓和黄房子走去。

===Original===

For some time we waited about casually, but nothing occurred. At length Kennedy walked up to the shed. The door was closed and double padlocked. He knocked, but there was no answer.

===Chinese===

我们装作无所事事地等了一阵，可什么动静也没有。末了，肯尼迪走上前去。门关着，挂着两把挂锁。他敲了敲门，无人应答。

===Original===

Just then a man appeared on the porch of the yellow house. Seeing us, he beckoned. As we approached he shouted, “He’s gone for the day!”

===Chinese===

恰在此时，黄房子的门廊上出现一个男人。他瞧见我们，招了招手。我们走近时，他喊道：「他今天走了，不回来了！」

===Original===

“Has he a city address﻿—any place I could reach him tonight?” asked Craig.

===Chinese===

「他在城里有地址吗——今晚有哪儿能找到他？」克雷格问。

===Original===

“I don’t know. He hired the barn from me for two weeks and paid in advance. He told me if I wanted to address him the best way was ‘Dr. K. Lamar, General Delivery, New York City.’ ”

===Chinese===

「不知道。他租我这谷仓租了两个星期，租金预付。他告诉我，我要是想给他寄信，最好的写法是『纽约市留局待取，K·拉马尔医生（Dr. K. Lamar）收』。」

===Original===

“Ah, then I suppose I had better write to him,” said Kennedy, apparently much gratified to learn the name. “I presume he’ll be taking away his apparatus soon?”

===Chinese===

「啊，那我最好还是写信给他，」肯尼迪说，得了这个名字，看样子喜出望外。「我想，他不久就会把他的设备搬走吧？」

===Original===

“Can’t say. There’s enough of it. Cy Smith﻿—he’s in the electric light company up to the village﻿—says the doctor has used a powerful lot of current. He’s good pay, though he’s awful closemouthed. Flying’s over for today, ain’t it? Was that feller much hurt?”

===Chinese===

「说不好。那家什可海了去了。村里的赛·史密斯（Cy Smith）——他在电灯公司做事——说这位医生用的电流大得吓人。他倒不欠钱，就是嘴紧得要命。今天的飞行完事了，是吧？刚才那家伙伤得重不重？」

===Original===

“No, he’ll be all right tomorrow. I think he’ll fly again. The machine’s in pretty good condition. He’s bound to win that prize. Goodbye.”

===Chinese===

「不重，明天就没事了。我看他还会飞。机器的情况也不坏。那个奖他是拿定了。再见。」

===Original===

As he walked away I remarked, “How do you know Norton will fly again?”

===Chinese===

他走开后，我说：「你怎么知道诺顿还会再飞？」

===Original===

“I don’t,” answered Kennedy, “but I think that either he or Humphreys will. I wanted to see that this Lamar believes it anyhow. By the way, Walter, do you think you could grab a wire here and phone in a story to the *Star* that Norton isn’t much hurt and will probably be able to fly tomorrow? Try to get the City News Association, too, so that all the papers will have it. I don’t care about risking the general delivery﻿—perhaps Lamar won’t call for any mail, but he certainly will read the papers. Put it in the form of an interview with Norton﻿—I’ll see that it is all right and that there is no comeback. Norton will stand for it when I tell him my scheme.”

===Chinese===

「我不知道，」肯尼迪答道，「但我想不是他就是汉弗莱斯，总有一个会飞。我要让这位拉马尔信以为真。对了，沃尔特，你看你能不能在这儿抓条电话线，给《明星报》传一条消息，就说诺顿伤势不重，明天多半还能飞？再设法通知城市新闻社（City News Association），让各家报纸都能登出来。留局待取那头我不想冒险——拉马尔也许不会去取信，可报纸他是一定看的。把它写成对诺顿的专访——我担保不会有差错，也不会有后患。等我把计策告诉诺顿，他会认可的。」

===Original===

I caught the *Star* just in time for the last edition, and some of the other papers that had later editions also had the story. Of course all the morning papers had it.

===Chinese===

我赶在《明星报》末版截稿前把稿子发了出去，另外几家版期较晚的报纸也登了。当然，第二天各家晨报全都有这条消息。

===Original===

Norton spent the night in the Mineola Hospital. He didn’t really need to stay, but the doctor said it would be best in case some internal injury had been overlooked. Meanwhile Kennedy took charge of the hangar where the injured machine was. The men had been in a sort of panic; Humphrey could not be found, and the only reason, I think, why the two mechanicians stayed was because something was due them on their pay.

===Chinese===

诺顿在米尼奥拉医院（Mineola Hospital）过了夜。其实他大可不必住院，但医生说，万一有什么内伤被漏诊了，还是住下稳妥。与此同时，肯尼迪接管了停放受伤飞机的那座机库。伙计们已经有点人心惶惶；汉弗莱斯不知去向；两个机械师之所以留下，我想，唯一的原因是工资还有一笔没结清。

===Original===

Kennedy wrote them out personal checks for their respective amounts, but dated them two days ahead to insure their staying. He threw off all disguise now and with authority from Norton directed the repairing of the machine. Fortunately it was in pretty good condition. The broken part was the skids, not the essential parts of the machine. As for the gyroscope, there were plenty of them and another dynamo, and it was a very simple thing to replace the old one that had been destroyed.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪按各人应得的数目给他们开了私人支票，却把日期往后填了两天，好稳住他们。此刻他抛开了全部伪装，凭诺顿的授权指挥起飞机的修理。所幸机器坏得不算重：断的是滑橇，不是要害部件。至于陀螺仪，备货多的是，发电机也另有一台，把报废的那台换下来，不过是举手之劳。

===Original===

Sinclair worked with a will, far past his regular hours. Jaurette also worked, though one could hardly say with a will. In fact, most of the work was done by Sinclair and Kennedy, with Jaurette sullenly grumbling, mostly in French under his breath. I did not like the fellow and was suspicious of him. I thought I noticed that Kennedy did not allow him to do much of the work, either, though that may have been for the reason that Kennedy never asked anyone to help him who seemed unwilling.

===Chinese===

辛克莱干得起劲，早过了下班的钟点还在干。若雷特也在干，不过很难说是起劲。实际上，活儿大部分是辛克莱和肯尼迪干的，若雷特只是阴沉着脸嘟嘟囔囔，多半用法语压着嗓子嘀咕。我不喜欢这家伙，对他存着戒心。我似乎还留意到，肯尼迪也不让他沾多少活——不过那也许是因为，凡是不情愿的人，肯尼迪从不勉强他们相帮。

===Original===

“There,” exclaimed Craig about ten o’clock. “If we want to get back to the city in any kind of time tonight we had better quit. Sinclair, I think you can finish repairing these skids in the morning.”

===Chinese===

「行了，」十点钟光景克雷格说道。「今晚要想赶个像样的钟点回城，咱们最好收工。辛克莱，这几个滑橇，我看你明早再修完吧。」

===Original===

We locked up the hangar and hurried across to the station. It was late when we arrived in New York, but Kennedy insisted on posting off up to his laboratory, leaving me to run down to the *Star* office to make sure that our story was all right for the morning papers.

===Chinese===

我们锁好机库，急急赶到车站。到纽约时已经很晚，肯尼迪却坚持要赶到实验室去，让我跑一趟《明星报》馆，确认我们的稿子供晨报发排没有差错。

===Original===

I did not see him until morning, when a large touring-car drove up. Kennedy routed me out of bed. In the tonneau of the car was a huge package carefully wrapped up.

===Chinese===

直到第二天早晨，一辆大型游览车驶到门前，我才见到他。肯尼迪把我从床上薅了起来。车后座里放着一件裹得严严实实的大东西。

===Original===

“Something I worked on for a couple of hours last night,” explained Craig, patting it. “If this doesn’t solve the problem then I’ll give it up.”

===Chinese===

「昨晚我捣鼓了几个钟头弄出来的家伙，」克雷格拍拍它解释道。「这个要还解决不了问题，我就只好撒手了。」

===Original===

I was burning with curiosity, but somehow, by a perverse association of ideas, I merely reproached Kennedy for not taking enough rest.

===Chinese===

我好奇得心痒难熬，可不知怎么鬼使神差，岔开的念头占了上风，我只顾数落肯尼迪没好好休息。

===Original===

“Oh,” he smiled. “If I hadn’t been working last night, Walter, I couldn’t have rested at all for thinking about it.”

===Chinese===

「嗐，」他笑了。「沃尔特，昨晚我要是不干那点活，光在心里惦记，反倒一刻也歇不安生。」

===Original===

When we arrived at the field Norton was already there with his head bandaged. I thought him a little pale, but otherwise all right. Jaurette was sulking, but Sinclair had finished the repairs and was busily engaged in going over every bolt and wire. Humphreys had sent word that he had another offer and had not shown up.

===Chinese===

我们赶到飞行场时，诺顿已经到了，头上缠着绷带。我觉得他脸色有些苍白，旁的倒无恙。若雷特在生闷气，辛克莱却已修完了滑橇，正忙着把每一颗螺栓、每一根钢线挨个查过。汉弗莱斯捎话说另有了高就，没露面。

===Original===

“We must find him,” exclaimed Kennedy. “I want him to make a flight today. His contract calls for it.”

===Chinese===

「必须找到他，」肯尼迪说。「我要他今天飞。合同上写着的。」

===Original===

“I can do it, Kennedy,” asserted Norton. “See, I’m all right.”

===Chinese===

「我就能飞，肯尼迪，」诺顿坚持道。「瞧，我好好的。」

===Original===

He picked up two pieces of wire and held them at arm’s length, bringing them together, tip to tip, in front of him just to show us how he could control his nerves.

===Chinese===

他拿起两截铁丝，双臂平举，在身前一寸一寸凑拢，直到尖端对尖端地碰上——只为让我们看看他的神经有多稳。

===Original===

“And I’ll be better yet by this afternoon,” he added. “I can do that stunt with the points of pins then.”

===Chinese===

「到今天下午我还会更稳，」他补了一句。「到时候拿大头针的针尖，我也能来这么一手。」

===Original===

Kennedy shook his head gravely, but Norton insisted, and finally Kennedy agreed to give up wasting time trying to locate Humphreys. After that he and Norton had a long whispered conference in which Kennedy seemed to be unfolding a scheme.

===Chinese===

肯尼迪郑重地摇头，可诺顿执意如此，最后肯尼迪只得答应，不再浪费时间去找汉弗莱斯。随后他和诺顿咬了半天耳朵，看样子是在面授一条计策。

===Original===

“I understand,” said Norton at length, “you want me to put this sheet-lead cover over the dynamo and battery first. Then you want me to take the cover off, and also to detach the gyroscope, and to fly without using it. Is that it?”

===Chinese===

「我明白了，」末了诺顿说，「您要我先把这块铅皮罩罩在发电机和电池上。然后您要我摘掉罩子，卸下陀螺仪，不带它飞行。是这么回事吧？」

===Original===

“Yes,” assented Craig. “I will be on the roof of the grand stand. The signal will be three waves of my hat repeated till I see you get it.”

===Chinese===

「正是，」克雷格点头道。「我就在大看台顶上。信号是我挥帽三下，反复挥，直到我看见你收到为止。」

===Original===

After a quick luncheon we went up to our vantage-point. On the way Kennedy had spoken to the head of the Pinkertons engaged by the management for the meet, and had also dropped in to see the wireless operator to ask him to send up a messenger if he saw the same phenomena as he had observed the day before.

===Chinese===

匆匆吃过午饭，我们登上自己的瞭望点。路上肯尼迪已同大会雇来的平克顿（Pinkerton）侦探的头目打过招呼，还顺道去看了无线电报务员，请他一发现前一天那种现象，就立刻派人上来报信。

===Original===

On the roof Kennedy took from his pocket a little instrument with a needle which trembled back and forth over a dial. It was nearing the time for the start of the day’s flying, and the aeroplanes were getting ready. Kennedy was calmly biting a cigar, casting occasional glances at the needle as it oscillated. Suddenly, as Williams rose in the Wright machine, the needle swung quickly and pointed straight at the aviation field, vibrating through a small area, back and forth.

===Chinese===

到了看台顶上，肯尼迪从口袋里掏出一台小仪器，上面有根指针，在刻度盘上颤来颤去。当天飞行开始的时刻快到了，各架飞机都在准备。肯尼迪悠悠地咬着一支雪茄，不时瞥一眼那左右摆动的指针。忽然，随着威廉斯驾着莱特式飞机升空，指针猛地一甩，直指飞行场的方向，在一小块区间里来回抖动。

===Original===

“The operator is getting his apparatus ready to signal to Williams,” remarked Craig. “This is an apparatus called an ondometer. It tells you the direction and something of the magnitude of the Hertzian waves used in wireless.”

===Chinese===

「这是报务员在调设备，准备给威廉斯发信号，」克雷格说。「这台仪器叫电波计（ondometer），能告诉你无线电所用赫兹波（Hertzian waves）的方位，还能约略测出它的强度。」

===Original===

Five or ten minutes passed. Norton was getting ready to fly. I could see through my field glass that he was putting something over his gyroscope and over the dynamo, but could not quite make out what it was. His machine seemed to leap up in the air as if eager to redeem itself. Norton with his white-bandaged head was the hero of the hour. No sooner had his aeroplane got up over the level of the trees than I heard a quick exclamation from Craig.

===Chinese===

五分钟，十分钟过去了。诺顿准备起飞了。透过望远镜，我看得见他往陀螺仪上、发电机上罩了点什么，只是辨不真切。他的飞机蹿上空中，仿佛急着想一雪前耻。缠着白色绷带的诺顿成了当红的英雄。他的飞机刚一升到树梢之上，我就听见克雷格短促地惊呼一声。

===Original===

“Look at the needle, Walter!” he cried. “As soon as Norton got into the air it shot around directly opposite to the wireless station, and now it is pointing﻿—”

===Chinese===

「看指针，沃尔特！」他喊道。「诺顿一升空，它就刷地转了个向，同无线电台正好相反——这会儿它指的是——」

===Original===

We raised our eyes in the direction which it indicated. It was precisely in line with the weather-beaten barn.

===Chinese===

我们顺着指针所示的方向抬眼望去。那方向恰好正对着那座饱经风雨的谷仓。

===Original===

I gasped. What did it mean? Did it mean in some way another accident to Norton﻿—perhaps fatal this time? Why had Kennedy allowed him to try it today when there was even a suspicion that some nameless terror was abroad in the air? Quickly I turned to see if Norton was all right. Yes, there he was, circling above us in a series of wide spirals, climbing up, up. Now he seemed almost to stop, to hover motionless. He was motionless. His engine had been cut out, and I could see his propeller stopped. He was riding as a ship rides on the ocean.

===Chinese===

我倒抽一口气。这是什么意思？难道说诺顿又要大祸临头——这一回没准是杀身之祸？明明已有风声，说空中有某种无名的恐怖在游荡，肯尼迪为什么还容他今天冒险？我急忙回头看诺顿是否无恙。是啊，他正在我们头顶上一圈圈大螺旋地兜着，越爬越高。此刻他仿佛几乎停住了，悬在半空一动不动。他是真的静止了。引擎已经熄火，我看得出螺旋桨也停了。他稳稳地浮着，宛如一艘船浮在大海上。

===Original===

A boy ran up the ladder to the roof. Kennedy unfolded the note and shoved it into my hands. It was from the operator.

===Chinese===

一个孩子顺着梯子跑上看台顶。肯尼迪展开字条，塞进我手里。是报务员送来的。

===Original===

“Wireless out of business again. Curse that fellow who is butting in. Am keeping record,” was all it said.

===Chinese===

「无线电又瘫了。诅咒那个硬插进来的家伙。记录照作。」上面就这一句。

===Original===

I shot a glance of inquiry at Kennedy, but he was paying no attention now to anything but Norton. He held his watch in his hand.

===Chinese===

我探询地瞟了肯尼迪一眼，可他此刻除了诺顿什么都顾不上了。他手里握着怀表。

===Original===

“Walter,” he ejaculated as he snapped it shut, “it has now been seven minutes and a half since he stopped his propeller. The Brooks Prize calls for five minutes only. Norton has exceeded it fifty percent. Here goes.”

===Chinese===

「沃尔特，」他啪地合上表盖，脱口说道，「他停桨到现在已经七分半钟了。布鲁克斯奖只要求五分钟。诺顿超出了一半。看我的。」

===Original===

With his hat in his hand he waved three times and stopped. Then he repeated the process.

===Chinese===

他帽子拿在手里，挥了三下，停住。然后又挥了三下。

===Original===

At the third time the aeroplane seemed to give a start. The propeller began to revolve, Norton starting it on the compression successfully. Slowly he circled down again. Toward the end of the descent he stopped the engine and volplaned, or coasted, to the ground, landing gently in front of his hangar.

===Chinese===

到第三轮信号，那飞机仿佛一激灵。螺旋桨转了起来——诺顿借着汽缸压缩，一次点火成功。他缓缓地兜着圈子又降下来。降落临近终点时，他关了引擎，凭滑翔——也就是溜下去——轻轻落在自己机库前面。

===Original===

A wild cheer rose into the air from the crowd below us. All eyes were riveted on the activity about Norton’s biplane. They were doing something to it. Whatever it was, it was finished in a minute and the men were standing again at a respectful distance from the propellers. Again Norton was in the air. As he rose above the field Kennedy gave a last glance at his ondometer and sprang down the ladder. I followed closely. Back of the crowd he hurried, down the walk to the entrance near the railroad station. The man in charge of the Pinkertons was at the gate with two other men, apparently waiting.

===Chinese===

我们脚下的观众爆发出一阵冲天的欢呼。所有的目光都钉在诺顿双翼机旁那阵忙碌上。人们在往飞机上做什么。不管是什么，一分钟就弄完了，机械师们又站回离螺旋桨远远的地方。诺顿再次腾空。他升上场地之际，肯尼迪最后看了一眼电波计，翻身跳下梯子。我紧随其后。他绕到人群背后，顺着步道直奔火车站附近的入口。平克顿侦探的领班正带着另外两个人守在门口，显然是在等候。

===Original===

“Come on!” shouted Craig.

===Chinese===

「来吧！」克雷格喊道。

===Original===

We four followed him as fast as we could. He turned in at the lane running up to the yellow house, so as to approach the barn from the rear, unobserved.

===Chinese===

我们四个人跟定了他，脚下使足全力。他拐进通向黄房子的小巷，为的是从背后摸近谷仓，不叫人察觉。

===Original===

“Quietly, now,” he cautioned.

===Chinese===

「都轻一点。」他叮嘱道。

===Original===

We were now at the door of the barn. A curious crackling, snapping noise issued. Craig gently tried the door. It was bolted on the inside. As many of us as could threw ourselves like a human catapult against it. It yielded.

===Chinese===

我们已到谷仓门前。里头传出一阵古怪的噼啪爆裂声。克雷格轻轻推了推门。门从里面闩上了。我们几个人能挤上的都挤上去，像一具人肉撞门槌朝门撞去。门屈服了。

===Original===

Inside I saw a sheet of flame fifteen or twenty feet long﻿—it was a veritable artificial bolt of lightning. A man with a telescope had been peering out of the window, but now was facing us in surprise.

===Chinese===

屋内，我看见一条足有十五二十英尺长的火舌——那真是一道人造的闪电。一个手持望远镜的男人本来正伏在窗口窥望，此刻却惊愕地面朝着我们。

===Original===

“Lamar,” shouted Kennedy, drawing a pistol, “one motion of your hand and you are a dead man. Stand still where you are. You are caught red-handed.”

===Chinese===

「拉马尔，」肯尼迪拔出手枪大喝一声，「你的手敢动一下，你就死定了。站在原地别动。你被人赃并获了。」

===Original===

The rest of us shrank back in momentary fear of the gigantic forces of nature which seemed let loose in the room. The thought, in my mind at least, was: Suppose this archfiend should turn his deadly power on us?

===Chinese===

我们其余的人一时悚然后退，害怕那仿佛在屋里挣脱出来的天地伟力。至少我心里想的是：倘若这个魔王把那致命的力量对准我们，可怎么办？

===Original===

Kennedy saw us from the corner of his eye. “Don’t be afraid,” he said with just a curl to his lip. “I’ve seen all this before. It won’t hurt you. It’s a high frequency current. The man has simply appropriated the invention of Mr. Nikola Tesla. Seize him. He won’t struggle. I’ve got him covered.”

===Chinese===

肯尼迪用眼角瞟见我们。「别怕，」他嘴角微微一挑，说道。「这套把戏我早见识过。它伤不着你们。这是高频电流（high frequency current）。这家伙无非是盗用了尼古拉·特斯拉先生（Mr. Nikola Tesla）的发明。抓他。他不会反抗。我的枪口罩着他呢。」

===Original===

Two burly Pinkertons leaped forward gingerly into the midst of the electrical apparatus, and in less time than it takes to write it Lamar was hustled out to the doorway, each arm pinioned back of him.

===Chinese===

两名彪形大汉的平克顿侦探小心地蹿进电气设备中间，说时迟那时快，拉马尔已被架到门口，两条胳膊都反剪在了身后。

===Original===

As we stood, half dazed by the suddenness of the turn of events, Kennedy hastily explained:

===Chinese===

我们站在那里，被这突如其来的变故弄得半晌发蒙，肯尼迪匆匆解释道：

===Original===

“Tesla’s theory is that under certain conditions the atmosphere, which is normally a high insulator, assumes conducting properties and so becomes capable of conveying any amount of electrical energy. I myself have seen electrical oscillations such as these in this room of such intensity that while they could be circulated with impunity through one’s arms and chest they would melt wires farther along in the circuit. Yet the person through whom such a current is passing feels no inconvenience. I have seen a loop of heavy copper wire energised by such oscillations and a mass of metal within the loop heated to the fusing point, and yet into the space in which this destructive aerial turmoil was going on I have repeatedly thrust my hand and even my head, without feeling anything or experiencing any injurious aftereffect. In this form all the energy of all the dynamos of Niagara could pass through one’s body and yet produce no injury. But, diabolically directed, this vast energy has been used by this man to melt the wires in the little dynamo that runs Norton’s gyroscope. That is all. Now to the aviation field. I have something more to show you.”

===Chinese===

「特斯拉的理论是：在某些条件下，通常高度绝缘的大气会呈现出导电性，从而能够输送任意大的电能。我自己就见过像这屋里这样的电振荡，强度大到如此地步：它们安然无恙地通过人的双臂和胸膛，却能把电路再往前的电线熔化。而这样的电流穿过人身，那人竟毫无不适之感。我见过一圈粗铜线被这种振荡激励，线圈中的一块金属被加热到熔点；然而对这场毁灭性的空中骚动正酣的空间，我一再把手、甚至把头伸进去，毫无感觉，也毫无后患。以这种形态，尼亚加拉全部发电机的一切能量尽可以从人的身体通过而不造成任何伤害。可是，这股庞大的能量经此人恶魔般的驾驭，被用来熔化驱动诺顿陀螺仪的那台小发电机里的电线了。就是这么回事。现在去飞行场。我还有东西给你们看。」

===Original===

We hurried as fast as we could up the street and straight out on the field, across toward the Norton hangar, the crowd gaping in wonderment. Kennedy waved frantically for Norton to come down, and Norton, who was only a few hundred feet in the air, seemed to see and understand.

===Chinese===

我们顺着大街拼命赶到飞行场，径直穿过场地奔向诺顿的机库，人群看得目瞪口呆。肯尼迪发疯般挥手叫诺顿下来，而正在几百英尺高空的诺顿似乎看见了，也明白了。

===Original===

As we stood waiting before the hangar Kennedy could no longer restrain his impatience.

===Chinese===

我们在机库前站定等候，肯尼迪再也按捺不住。

===Original===

“I suspected some wireless-power trick when I found that the field wireless telegraph failed to work every time Norton’s aeroplane was in the air,” he said, approaching close to Lamar. “I just happened to catch sight of that peculiar wireless mast of yours. A little flash of light first attracted my attention to it. I thought it was an electric spark, but you are too clever for that, Lamar. Still, you forgot a much simpler thing. It was the glint of the sun on the lens of your telescope as you were watching Norton that betrayed you.”

===Chinese===

「我发现每当诺顿的飞机升空，场上的无线电台就失灵，就疑心有人在玩无线电输电的把戏，」他说着，逼近拉马尔。「我恰好瞥见了你那根形迹可疑的无线天线杆。先是一点闪光引起了我的注意。我起先当那是电火花——可你没那麼粗心，拉马尔。不过，你忘了简单得多的一件事：正是你窥望诺顿时，太阳照在望远镜镜片上的反光，出卖了你。」

===Original===

Lamar said nothing.

===Chinese===

拉马尔一言不发。

===Original===

“I’m glad to say you had no confederate in the hangar here,” continued Craig. “At first I suspected it. Anyhow, you succeeded pretty well single handed, two lives lost and two machines wrecked. Norton flew all right yesterday when he left his gyroscope and dynamo behind, but when he took them along you were able to fuse the wires in the dynamo﻿—you pretty nearly succeeded in adding his name to those of Browne and Herrick.”

===Chinese===

「令人欣慰的是，这机库里你没有内应，」克雷格接着说。「起初我还疑心过。不管怎样，你单枪匹马干得也算漂亮——两条人命，两架飞机。昨天诺顿把陀螺仪和发电机留下再飞，就安然无恙；可他一带上它们，你就能把发电机里的电线熔住——差一点，布朗和赫里克的名字后面就得添上他的名字了。」

===Original===

The whir of Norton’s machine told us he was approaching. We scattered to give him space enough to choose the spot where he would alight. As the men caught his machine to steady it, he jumped lightly to the ground.

===Chinese===

诺顿机器的呼啸声告诉我们他来了。我们散开来，给他留足挑选降落地点的余地。机械师们扶住飞机稳住它时，他轻盈地跳到地上。

===Original===

“Where’s Kennedy?” he asked, and then, without waiting for a reply, he exclaimed: “Queerest thing I ever saw up there. The dynamo wasn’t protected by the sheet-lead shield in this flight as in the first today. I hadn’t risen a hundred feet before I happened to hear the darndest sputtering in the dynamo. Look, boys, the insulation is completely burned off the wires, and the wires are nearly all fused together.”

===Chinese===

「肯尼迪呢？」他问，随即不等回答便嚷了起来：「我在上头见到了一辈子没见过的怪事。这一趟发电机可不像今天头一趟那样有铅皮罩护着。我还没升上一百英尺，就听见发电机里噼里啪啦响得邪乎。瞧啊，伙计们，电线上的绝缘层烧了个精光，电线几乎全熔到一块儿了。」

===Original===

“So it was in the other two wrecked machines,” added Kennedy, coming coolly forward. “If you hadn’t had everything protected by those shields I gave you in your first flight today you would have simply repeated your fall of yesterday﻿—perhaps fatally. This fellow has been directing the full strength of his wireless high-tension electricity straight at you all the time.”

===Chinese===

「另外两架失事的飞机上也是如此，」肯尼迪冷静地上前补了一句。「今天头一趟飞行，要不是有我给你的那些罩子把一切护住，你无非是把昨天的坠落再重演一遍——没准就坠得没命了。这家伙一直在把他无线电高压电的全副威力，笔直地对准你。」

===Original===

“What fellow?” demanded Norton.

===Chinese===

「什么家伙？」诺顿追问。

===Original===

The two Pinkertons shoved Lamar forward. Norton gave a contemptuous look at him. “Delanne,” he said, “I knew you were a crook when you tried to infringe on my patent, but I didn’t think you were coward enough to resort to﻿—to murder.”

===Chinese===

两名平克顿侦探把拉马尔往前一推。诺顿轻蔑地瞥了他一眼。「德兰，」他说，「你侵犯我专利那会儿，我就知道你是个无赖，可我没想到你竟下作到——下作到谋杀的地步。」

===Original===

Lamar, or rather Delanne, shrank back as if even the protection of his captors was safety compared to the threatening advance of Norton toward him.

===Chinese===

拉马尔——或者说德兰——直往后缩，仿佛比起诺顿逼上前来的架势，连擒获他的人手里的看管也算得上安稳了。

===Original===

“Pouff!” exclaimed Norton, turning suddenly on his heel. “What a fool I am! The law will take care of such scoundrels as you. What’s the grand stand cheering for now?” he asked, looking across the field in an effort to regain his self-control.

===Chinese===

「呸！」诺顿猛地转过脚跟，啐了一声。「我真是个傻子！你这种恶棍，自有法律来收拾。大看台这会儿欢呼什么呢？」他望着场地那头问道，竭力让自己定下神来。

===Original===

A boy from one of the hangars down the line spoke up from the back of the crowd in a shrill, piping voice. “You have been awarded the Brooks Prize, sir,” he said.

===Chinese===

排在后面的一座机库里的一个孩子，从人群后面尖着嗓子开了口。「先生，布鲁克斯奖颁给您了。」他说。
